# State renaming scripted effects
# Fire on a state scope to rename the state and its VPs

clear_state_rename_flags = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = state_name_roqualian }
		clr_state_flag = state_name_roqualian
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = state_name_katzen }
		clr_state_flag = state_name_katzen
	}
}
